- **nanopayment-x402**: Access AIsa x402-paid /apis/v2/ endpoints using Arc testnet USDC and Circle Gateway. Use when setting up x402 payments, creating or funding an Arc wallet, depositing into Circle Gateway, picking the right AIsa endpoint for a task, estimating per-call cost, or making paid AIsa API calls without an API key. 104 endpoints across Twitter, Financial, Search, Scholar, Perplexity, YouTube, and CoinGecko categories. ([Christian-Stefan/Hackathon-Democratization-of-asset-exchange-by-using-Agentic-AI-and-DeFI-technologies](https://github.com/Christian-Stefan/Hackathon-Democratization-of-asset-exchange-by-using-Agentic-AI-and-DeFI-technologies/tree/HEAD/AISA/nanopayment-x402))
- **nanopb-integration**: Use when integrating, porting, configuring, or debugging nanopb Protocol Buffers, .proto generation, pb_encode, pb_decode, callbacks, fixed sizes, or embedded serialization ([easyzoom/aix-skills](https://github.com/easyzoom/aix-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/nanopb-integration) · ★39)
- **nanopdf**: **Version:** 1.0.0 **Domain:** document ([br3eze-code/br3eze-code](https://github.com/br3eze-code/br3eze-code/tree/HEAD/skills/nanopdf) · ★3)
- **nanophotonet-pinn-inverse-design**: Physics-informed AI-driven inverse design framework for nonlinear metasurfaces using hybrid CNN-autoencoder architecture ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/nanophotonet-pinn-inverse-design) · ★4)
- **nanophysicist**: Think and work like an expert Nanophysicist. Use when a task calls for Nanophysicist judgment. Reasons from quantum confinement dimensionality, Coulomb diamonds and SET conditions, Kondo vs Luttinger-liquid power laws, and lock-in cryostat transport through STM/STS, AFM/KPFM, and STEM/EELS while treating charging artifacts, tip convolution, contact resistance, and beam-damage plasmon shifts as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/nanophysicist/skills/nanophysicist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **nanophysicist**: Expert-thinking profile for Nanophysicist (experimental / low-T transport / SPM / electron spectroscopy): Reasons from quantum confinement dimensionality, Coulomb diamonds and SET conditions, Kondo vs Luttinger-liquid power laws, and lock-in cryostat transport through STM/STS, AFM/KPFM, and STEM/EELS while treating charging artifacts, tip convolution, contact resistance, and beam-damage plasmon shifts as first-class... ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/nanophysicist) · ★13)
- **nanophysicist**: Reasons from quantum confinement dimensionality, Coulomb diamonds and SET conditions, Kondo vs Luttinger-liquid power laws, and lock-in cryostat transport through STM/STS, AFM/KPFM, and STEM/EELS while treating charging artifacts, tip convolution, contact resistance, and beam-damage plasmon shifts as first-class failure modes. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/nanophysicist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **nanophysicist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for nanophysicist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/nanophysicist))
- **Nanophysicist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Nanophysicist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-nanophysicist))
- **nanoplot**: Generate diagnostic plots and machine-readable summary statistics for Oxford Nanopore or other long-read FASTQ, sequencing-summary, or BAM inputs with NanoPlot. Use for raw-versus-filtered read length, quality, yield, and alignment QC; not for filtering reads. ([PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills](https://github.com/PKU-EMBL/Metagenomics-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoplot) · ★7)
- **nanopore-5mc-modification-calling**: Use when profiling plant 5mC from ONT R10.4 POD5/BAM data, especially CHH methylation in repetitive regions (centromeres, transposable elements) where short-read BS-seq coverage is poor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-5mc-modification-calling))
- **nanopore-5mcpg-detection-workflow**: Use when nanopore POD5 or FAST5 signal must be basecalled with move tables, aligned while preserving move tags, analysed for 5mCpG with DeepMod2, aggregated with modkit, and checked against orthogonal methylation measurements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/workflows/nanopore-5mcpg-detection))
- **nanopore-6ma-region-calling**: Use when you have nanopolish eventalign output from nanopore reads (e.g., nanoHiMe-seq HepG2 H3K27me3 data) and need to identify 6mA-containing genomic windows, optionally limited to ChIP-seq/CUT&Tag peak regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-6ma-region-calling))
- **nanopore-adapter-barcode-trimming**: Use when Nanopore FASTQ reads from rapid (e.g. SQK-RBK114-96) or native (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-adapter-barcode-trimming))
- **nanopore-adapter-trimming**: Use when raw Nanopore FASTQ reads enter a metagenome assembly workflow and adapters must be removed before control-read decontamination (NanoLyse), quality filtering (Filtlong) and long-read assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-adapter-trimming))
- **nanopore-alignment-target-setup**: Use when a user supplies a custom target FASTA (not a precompiled NanoPipe target) and a LAST sequence database plus MinION-tuned scoring parameters must be built before running lastal alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-alignment-target-setup))
- **nanopore-amplicon-pipeline-execution**: Use when an ONT- (or PacBio-) sequenced 10x Genomics 5' cDNA or targeted TCR amplicon FASTQ must be converted into per-cell-barcode TCR calls and a TCR-positive cell count comparable to Illumina-based 10x V(DJ) results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-amplicon-pipeline-execution))
- **nanopore-amplicon-read-subsampling**: Decision support for choosing how many ONT MinION reads (2D or 1D2) to allocate per OTU when constructing de novo 16S rRNA consensus sequences, or when validating that a consensus depth yields sufficient accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-amplicon-read-subsampling))
- **nanopore-bam-coverage-profiling**: Use when putative DEL/DUP SV calls (from Sniffles, CuteSV, SVIM, NanoVar on nanopore WGS alignments) must be profiled by per-base coverage across each SV interval plus 1-Mb up- and downstream flanks before coverage-based filtering or truth-set validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-bam-coverage-profiling))
- **nanopore-barcode-annotation**: Use when Nanopore FASTQ reads need demultiplexing or barcode attachment auditing (rapid or native kits like SQK-RBK114-96/SQK-NBD114-96), or when detecting complex/fused barcode configurations that simpler demultiplexers mis-handle. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-barcode-annotation))
- **nanopore-barcode-assignment-threshold-tuning**: Use when demultiplexing Nanopore FASTQ reads with Barbell and you need to reproduce, verify, or tune barcode acceptance scoring: e.g., validating that reads are assigned their synthetically embedded barcode under the &gt;=20%-of-perfect-score and &gt;=10%-separation default thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-barcode-assignment-threshold-tuning))
- **nanopore-barcode-kit-generation**: Use when designing or expanding a DNA barcode kit for nanopore sequencing at a fixed barcode length (e.g., 24 bp) and error tolerance (e.g., 2 edit errors), or when comparing a generated kit's demultiplexing accuracy to official ONT kits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-barcode-kit-generation))
- **nanopore-barcode-sequence-search**: Use when validating whether submitted GenBank/NCBI assemblies (e.g. CP131573.1, CP165501.1) carry detectable Nanopore rapid-barcode artefacts from kits like SQK-RBK114-96. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-barcode-sequence-search))
- **nanopore-basecalling-error-feature-analysis**: Use when quantifying per-site m6A stoichiometry from ONT direct RNA reads that have wt, fully modified, and unmodified (knockout-derived) counterparts, using base-called error features rather than raw current intensity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-basecalling-error-feature-analysis))
- **nanopore-basecalling-with-dorado**: Use when the starting point is a folder of ONT POD5 files (direct RNA, cDNA, or genomic DNA) that must be basecalled, optionally with modified bases, into a single unmapped BAM before alignment or modkit analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-basecalling-with-dorado))
- **nanopore-cdna-alignment-with-minimap2-splice**: Use when analyzing 10x Oxford Nanopore long-read single-cell cDNA (or PacBio with an appropriate index) and reads must be aligned to a genome for downstream isoform reconstruction and gene quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-cdna-alignment-with-minimap2-splice))
- **nanopore-cell-barcode-umi-recovery**: Use when raw Nanopore FASTQ reads from 10x 3'/5' or Visium-barcoded libraries need cell-barcode/UMI extraction and error-robust UMI deduplication before isoform or gene quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-cell-barcode-umi-recovery))
- **nanopore-consensus-error-attribution**: Use when FASTA consensus reads from a nanopore amplicon workflow (INC-Seq / chopSeq / nanoClust) must be error-profiled against 16S references, e.g. to test whether remaining errors are homopolymer deletions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-consensus-error-attribution))
- **nanopore-consensus-genome-variant-calling**: Use when a nanopore virus-surveillance workflow has already reconstructed viral consensus genomes (reference-based or hybrid assembly) and you need to identify structural variants relative to the reference as an optional downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-consensus-genome-variant-calling))
- **nanopore-consensus-polishing**: Use when reconstructing a consensus genome for a known virus from untargeted nanopore reads after a reference genome has been identified, and a polished reference-guided consensus (not de novo contigs) is the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-consensus-polishing))
- **nanopore-consensus-read-processing**: Use when handling INC-Seq consensus reads from ONT MinION amplicon sequencing (2D or 1D2 chemistry) that require fragmentation and orientation correction with chopSeq before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-consensus-read-processing))
- **nanopore-cpg-methylation-calling**: Use when evaluating whether a CpG methylation caller is robust across heterogeneous human/plant nanopore R10.4 datasets, or when calling per-read CpG methylation states from POD5 + sorted BAM data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-cpg-methylation-calling--fc9e9b4c299a1699))
- **nanopore-cpg-methylation-calling**: Use when nanopore FAST5/FASTQ data needs per-read CpG (5mC) methylation calls, especially alongside 6mA detection, or when comparing nanoHiMe vs nanopolish vs Megalodon accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-cpg-methylation-calling--0629a3089a562423))
- **nanopore-current-chunk-kmer-extraction**: Use when building a training dataset from ONT dRNA fast5/pod5 signal data and you must pair variable-length raw current chunks with their reference kmers to train a modification-detection model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-current-chunk-kmer-extraction))
- **nanopore-current-rescaling-and-event-assignment**: Use when raw ONT fastq/fast5 reads (basecalled with Albacore or another event-preserving basecaller) must be aligned and event-level, model-scaled current values extracted before per-read methylation classification with mCaller. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-current-rescaling-and-event-assignment))
- **nanopore-current-signal-assembly**: Use when a task requires extracting the entire raw current measurement trace of a specific read ID from a POD5 file (e.g. for downstream numeric analysis or figure export) while keeping the reading process responsive, mirroring pod5Viewer's 'View signal' and 'Plot signal' behavior. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-current-signal-assembly))
- **nanopore-direct-rna-basecalling**: Use when raw nanopore fast5 dRNA-Seq reads (HEK293 WT/KO, Arabidopsis col0/vir1, or synthetic RL/SL oligos) must be converted to basecalled sequences and hidden features as the first step of an mAFiA m6A workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-direct-rna-basecalling))
- **nanopore-direct-rna-m6a-model-training**: Use when you have RedNano-extracted read-level features (k_signals_rect, qual, mis, ins, dele columns) from modified and unmodified Nanopore DRS samples and need to train a species- or dataset-specific m6A detection model instead of using a pre-trained one. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-direct-rna-m6a-model-training))
- **nanopore-drna-basecalling**: Use when raw ONT dRNA fast5/pod5 signals from SQK-RNA002 or SQK-RNA004 chemistries must be basecalled while simultaneously detecting modified bases (I, m6A, m5C, Psi) without re-squiggling or separate modification models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-drna-basecalling))
- **nanopore-drna-modification-aware-basecalling**: Use when raw ONT dRNA fast5/pod5 files (SQK-RNA002 or SQK-RNA004) must be basecalled while simultaneously detecting modifications (e.g., inosine, m6A) without re-squiggling or separate modification-scoring models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-drna-modification-aware-basecalling))
- **nanopore-drna-quantification-output-parsing**: Use when you have LR-Kallisto long-read quantification output from Dogme-processed dRNA replicates and need to determine how many detected transcripts are known isoforms versus novel (ISM, NIC, NNC), and whether classification is reproducible across replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-drna-quantification-output-parsing))
- **nanopore-drna-seq-m6a-detection**: Use when base-called Nanopore fast5 dRNA-seq data (e.g. SK1 yeast, mouse, or human samples) must be profiled for m6A sites and detection tools need to be compared or applied under controlled thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-drna-seq-m6a-detection))
- **nanopore-drs-transcriptome-alignment**: Use when input is Oxford Nanopore direct-RNA FASTQ reads plus a transcriptome FASTA and the goal is to produce BAM alignments that preserve secondary mappings for EM-based isoform quantification (NanoCount, Salmon, LIQA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-drs-transcriptome-alignment))
- **nanopore-error-model-fault-injection**: Use when evaluating DNA storage pipeline designs under a channel-with-memory (nanopore) error model, specifically to test whether MSA-only pipelines can compete with HEDGES-MSA when errors are bursty rather than i.i.d. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-error-model-fault-injection))
- **nanopore-event-table-to-translocation-time-conversion**: Use when you have ONT FAST5 data basecalled with Albacore and need base-level translocation times in seconds — e.g. to build per-window TT quantiles for non-B DNA versus B-DNA comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-event-table-to-translocation-time-conversion))
- **nanopore-fast5-file-splitting**: Use when raw Nanopore DRS FAST5 files contain multiple read IDs (multi-read format) and downstream tools such as Tombo resquiggle or Guppy require per-read single FAST5 files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-fast5-file-splitting))
- **nanopore-fastq-quality-filtering**: Use when new basecalled FASTQ batches from an Oxford Nanopore MinION/Flongle run must be quality-filtered per batch, and per-batch QC statistics (before/after filtering read and base counts) are needed before taxonomic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-fastq-quality-filtering))
- **nanopore-feature-ablation**: Use when reproducing or extending ablation experiments on Nanoident's multi-column CNN to determine which nanopore feature or architectural component drives methylation typing and fine-mapping accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-feature-ablation))
- **nanopore-genome-assembly**: Use when assembling a large (hundreds of Mbp to multi-Gbp) eukaryotic genome from uncorrected nanopore FASTQ/FASTA reads on modest hardware, and existing de novo assemblers such as Canu are computationally prohibitive (tens of thousands of CPU hours). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-genome-assembly))
- **nanopore-long-amplicon-consensus-assembly**: Use when demultiplexed MinION long-amplicon (e.g. rDNA) reads must be converted into a per-specimen consensus sequence of sufficient accuracy to compare against an Illumina short-read reference, with a minimum per-position coverage cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-amplicon-consensus-assembly))
- **nanopore-long-read-alignment**: Use when long nanopore FASTQ reads from targeted (e.g. FUDGE-enriched) sequencing must be aligned to a human reference to support downstream SV/fusion calling with NanoSV, Sniffles or NanoFG. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-read-alignment))
- **nanopore-long-read-assembly**: Use when the input is Oxford Nanopore long-read FASTQ data (genomic, metagenomic, or plasmid) and a quick de novo assembly with error-corrected consensus is needed without command-line skills. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-read-assembly))
- **nanopore-long-read-isoform-alignment**: Use when quantifying isoforms or PSI from single-cell/spatially barcoded Nanopore long reads (e.g., CRCLM sample) and raw minimap2 alignments show misaligned small internal exons inflating false-positive isoforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-long-read-isoform-alignment))
- **nanopore-long-read-locus-subsetting**: Use when you need locus-restricted RNA editing or SNV rates from ScNaUmi-seq error-corrected consensus molecules, e.g. measuring A-to-I editing at two linked sites like Gria2 Q/R and R/G and testing whether editing at one site influences the other. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-read-locus-subsetting))
- **nanopore-long-read-methylation-pipeline**: Use when analyzing EMseq/nanoEM base-converted Oxford Nanopore long reads to quantify per-CpG methylation genome-wide, or when reproducing methylation rates and CpG coverage versus WGBS, EMseq, and nanopolish. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-read-methylation-pipeline))
- **nanopore-long-read-scaffolding-realtime**: Use when a SPAdes short-read draft assembly needs finishing with nanopore long reads, especially when streaming MinION data in real time or when minimizing long-read coverage (e.g. ~30-fold for E. coli, ~14-fold for Kpn2146 plasmids). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-read-scaffolding-realtime))
- **nanopore-long-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking or validating long-read isoform quantification pipelines where real data lack gold-standard isoform counts, and a simulated cell-by-isoform matrix with known error profiles is needed as ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-long-read-simulation))
- **nanopore-long-read-to-short-read-conversion**: Use when a nanopore FASTQ (e.g., .fq.gz from ONT) must be converted into NGS-liked short-read sets to run conventional NGS pipelines for hotspot mutation detection in a hotspot panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-long-read-to-short-read-conversion))
- **nanopore-m6a-per-read-classification**: Use when ONT fastq/fast5 reads (Albacore-basecalled, R9/R9.4/R9.5) need per-read m6A probability calls at known motif or positions-file sites, using nanopolish eventalign TSVs with --scale-events and a trained model like r95_twobase_model_NN_6_m6A.pkl. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-m6a-per-read-classification))
- **nanopore-metagenome-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking an ARG profiler (e.g., Argo) against assembly/metaFlye+Kraken2 or binning/SemiBin2+GTDB-Tk pipelines and ground-truth ARG abundances at controlled sequencing coverages are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-metagenome-read-simulation))
- **nanopore-metagenomic-classification-verification**: Use when a real-time Oxford Nanopore (MinION/Flongle) metagenomic run has been classified with Kraken2 and hits to user-defined species of interest or pathogens need secondary confirmation before alerting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-metagenomic-classification-verification))
- **nanopore-methylation**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/nanopore-methylation) · ★745)
- **nanopore-methylation**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/long-read-sequencing/nanopore-methylation) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **nanopore-methylation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when evaluating methylation-calling accuracy of a Nanopore 5mC caller against short-read bisulfite ground truth at per-read and per-site granularity, needing stratified F1 scores with explicit methylation-frequency filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-methylation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **nanopore-methylation-calling**: Use when calling 5mC methylation from ONT R10.4 POD5/BAM plant nanopore data in CpG, CHG, or CHH contexts, or when needing per-site methylation rates comparable to BS-seq across species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-methylation-calling--22f6449e0b138010))
- **nanopore-methylation-calling**: Use when raw nanopore fast5 files must be converted to per-read 5mC CpG calls or per-site methylation frequencies, especially to benchmark or combine tool predictions on R9.4.1 flowcell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-methylation-calling--ab6e4dfe90fd7ce1))
- **nanopore-methylation-feature-handling**: Use when raw Nanopore FAST5 signals must be turned into balanced, denoised training samples for 5mC detection (e.g. building DeepSignal-plant training sets with CpG/CHG/CHH motifs from re-squiggled reads). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-methylation-feature-handling))
- **nanopore-methylation-motif-enrichment**: Use when you need to de novo discover DNA methylation motifs (6mA, 5mC, 4mC) from native vs WGA nanopore signal data of a bacterium, and benchmark recovery against REBASE reference motifs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-methylation-motif-enrichment))
- **nanopore-mock-community-diversity-analysis**: Use when assessing whether direct 97% VSEARCH clustering of full-length post-chopSeq nanopore amplicon reads gives accurate OTU richness for mock communities, or when justifying a partition-based clustering step by quantifying singleton-driven OTU inflation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-mock-community-diversity-analysis))
- **nanopore-modification-calling-with-deepbam**: Use when nanopore R10.4 FASTQ/BAM/POD5 data need CpG methylation calling and per-site modification rates, especially when Dorado's methylation model shows inconsistent performance across heterogeneous human or plant datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-modification-calling-with-deepbam))
- **nanopore-modification-frequency-filtering**: Use when you have an aligned nanopore modBAM with MM/ML base-modification tags from direct RNA, cDNA, or genomic DNA reads and need high-confidence per-site modification calls filtered by coverage and modification frequency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-modification-frequency-filtering))
- **nanopore-modification-tool-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how accurately nanopore RNA modification callers (m6A/m5C) estimate site-level stoichiometry or sensitivity/FDR on known ground-truth mixtures, e.g. IVT sets with 20-100% modification levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-modification-tool-benchmarking))
- **nanopore-modified-basecalling**: Use when raw Nanopore fast5 single-read files must be converted into per-read or per-site CpG 5mC methylation calls, or when comparing methylation-calling tools against a WGBS/truth reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-modified-basecalling))
- **nanopore-motif-typing-evaluation**: Use when running Nanoident inference on bacterial Nanopore motif data and predicted methylation type (4mC/5mC/6mA) does not match the nucleotide at the predicted methylated position, requiring correction before accuracy evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-motif-typing-evaluation))
- **nanopore-native-rna-read-classification**: Use when you have native RNA Nanopore reads from a subcellular fraction (e.g. chromatin, nucleoplasm, cytoplasm) and need per-read premature vs. mature labels to estimate processing-state fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-native-rna-read-classification))
- **nanopore-probability-distribution-modeling**: Use when you have CHEUI-solo Model 1 per-read probability distributions grouped by transcript site and need site-level m6A/m5C modification probability and stoichiometry in a single condition without a knock-out/control sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-probability-distribution-modeling))
- **nanopore-raw-data-file-parsing**: Use when you need to enumerate every read stored in a POD5 file and extract each read's read_id, e.g. to build a read inventory, feed a filter list, or verify the number of reads a viewer reports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-data-file-parsing))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-alignment**: Use when performing signal-domain mapping of raw nanopore reads (BLOW5/FAST5) against a reference FASTA for selective sequencing (Read Until) or when benchmarking sDTW alignment throughput on CPU vs FPGA hardware. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-alignment))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-basecalling**: Use when you have raw nanopore FAST5 electrolytic-current signal files and need to decode them into per-read nucleotide sequences without prior signal segmentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-basecalling))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-basecalling**: Use when raw nanopore fast5 files must be translated into basecalled reads (FASTQ/FASTA) with a deep learning basecaller, either with a pretrained model (DNA_default/RNA_default) or a custom-trained model, and accuracy should rival Albacore v2.0.1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-basecalling))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-event-detection**: Use when raw nanopore signal reads (e.g., SARS-CoV-2 BLOW5 from Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.7314838) must be converted into per-read event sequences—event detection, prefix trimming, z-score normalization, and scaling—before squiggle mapping or sDTW-based Read Until on R9.4 chemistry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-event-detection))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-kmer-feature-encoding**: Use when resquiggled Nanopore DRS FAST5 signals and basecall alignments are available and per-read k-mer/signal/error features must be encoded and pooled into site-level m6A probabilities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-kmer-feature-encoding))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-mapping**: Use when the input is raw nanopore signal data (FAST5, POD5, SLOW5, or BLOW5) from legacy r9.4 chemistry that must be mapped to a reference genome in real time, especially for large genomes such as human NA12878. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-mapping))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-resquiggling**: Use when preparing Nanopore DRS FAST5 reads for RedNano feature extraction: after guppy basecalling (--fast5_out) and before extracting k-mer raw signals, the signals must be resquiggled against a reference transcript FASTA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-resquiggling))
- **nanopore-raw-signal-seed-hash-matching**: Use when mapping raw nanopore signal reads to a reference genome via hash-based seed matching, or when reproducing how RawHash converts matching seed hash values into a mapped genomic region through colinear chaining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-raw-signal-seed-hash-matching))
- **nanopore-read-alignment**: Use when aligning Oxford Nanopore MinION 1d/2d reads (or PacBio reads) to a reference genome with high sensitivity despite high error rates, or when downstream SV/SNV calling requires gapped alignments that span 100 bp–4 kbp insertion/deletion events directly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-alignment--c3fdf310d3fdf22c))
- **nanopore-read-alignment**: Use when ONT long-read FASTQ data (e.g. SQK-PCS111 cDNA, SQK-RNA002 dRNA, or SQK-LSK109 pDNA reads) must be mapped to a plasmid or mRNA reference as the first step of VAX-seq quality analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-alignment--8c5d9f9b2413e870))
- **nanopore-read-alignment-and-biotype-assignment**: Use when you have nanopore DRS reads from a RISER live run (even-numbered channels = deplete, others = control) and need per-transcript or per-biotype read-count and nucleotide-count changes to evaluate depletion/enrichment efficiency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-alignment-and-biotype-assignment))
- **nanopore-read-alignment-and-preprocessing**: Use when raw ONT reads must be converted into methylation-annotated primary-alignment BAMs (MM/ML tags) plus SNV-phased, haplotagged BAM/VCF inputs for methylation-based phasing of ~30X human autosomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-alignment-and-preprocessing))
- **nanopore-read-alignment-to-grch38**: Use when Dorado sup-basecalled ONT reads (kit v14, R10.4.1) from samples such as HG002/HG003/HG004 must be placed on GRCh38 as sorted, indexed BAMs before variant calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-alignment-to-grch38))
- **nanopore-read-alignment-to-reference-genome**: Use when raw nanopore PASS reads (e.g. PCR-amplified or M.SssI-treated E. coli K12 samples in fast5/fastq) must be mapped to a reference such as ecoli_k12_mg1655.fasta before methylation-calling or coverage filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-alignment-to-reference-genome))
- **nanopore-read-anchor-detection**: Use when a pipeline must convert coarse seed-hit clusters from a seeding stage into precise read/reference coordinate anchors prior to anchor chaining (LCSk), specifically for MinION nanopore or other long error-prone reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-anchor-detection))
- **nanopore-read-attribute-parsing**: Use when a POD5 nanopore file's per-read metadata must be fully enumerated and flattened — e.g. to inspect nested attributes of a selected read ID or export them to per-read JSON files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-attribute-parsing))
- **nanopore-read-basecalling**: Use when raw nanopore signals simulated from a reference (e.g. BLOW5/POD5 from seq2squiggle, squigulator, or DeepSimulator under R9.4.1 or R10.4.1 chemistry) must be turned into basecalled reads for variant-detection benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-basecalling))
- **nanopore-read-basecalling-and-resquiggling**: Use when starting from raw single-read FAST5 Nanopore files (e.g. R9.4 plant reads) that need basecalls and raw-signal-to-reference alignment before deep-learning methylation detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-basecalling-and-resquiggling))
- **nanopore-read-basecalling-orchestration**: Use when raw nanopore signal files (FAST5, .fast5.gz, POD5, .pod5.gz) or already-basecalled FASTQ (.fastq, .fq, .fastq.gz, .fq.gz) must enter VirDetector's preprocessing before taxonomic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-basecalling-orchestration))
- **nanopore-read-classification**: Use when base-called nanopore reads must be classified as target vs non-target (e.g., adaptive-sampling host/strain depletion) against one-genome or multi-strain pan-genome references where minimap2 alignment is too slow or memory-hungry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-classification))
- **nanopore-read-data-preprocessing**: Use when input data are ONT multi-read fast5 files that must be split into per-read single fast5 files before raw-signal tools like tombo resquiggle can process them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-data-preprocessing))
- **nanopore-read-de-novo-assembly**: Use when assembling untargeted (metagenomic) Oxford Nanopore FASTQ reads from clinical samples where uneven viral genome abundance (e.g., co-infections) may prevent a single Canu run from recovering low-abundance virus genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-de-novo-assembly))
- **nanopore-read-demultiplexing**: Use when demultiplexing Nanopore FASTQ reads from barcoding kits (e.g. SQK-RBK114-96 rapid or SQK-NBD114-96 native barcoding), especially when reads show complex barcode attachments, partially trimmed adapter contamination, or custom barcode patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-demultiplexing--e2129388e334215b))
- **nanopore-read-demultiplexing**: Use when you have dual-indexed MinION FASTA/FASTQ amplicon reads (e.g. long rDNA amplicons) plus a barcode/index combination file and need to assign each read to a specimen by matching forward/reverse barcodes and primers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-demultiplexing--214a5d7bc9574e91))
- **nanopore-read-demultiplexing**: Use when nanopore FASTQ/FASTA reads have been ligated with a PRO-designed barcode kit and each read must be assigned to its source barcode, especially when comparing accuracy against Guppy or edit-distance assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-demultiplexing--a85cc6bd56b5a966))
- **nanopore-read-downsampling**: Use when comparing methylation callers or basecalling models on nanopore data and you need standardized, depth-matched BAM inputs (e.g., 10x and 30x coverage) from high-coverage R10.4 datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-downsampling))
- **nanopore-read-end-trimming**: Use when raw Oxford Nanopore FASTQ reads contain PCR/tiling primer and adapter sequences at read ends and a fixed-length trim of both ends is needed before downstream classification or assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-end-trimming))
- **nanopore-read-error-correction**: Use when assembling genomes from noisy Oxford Nanopore long reads and you must choose between correct-then-assemble vs assemble-then-correct correction strategies, or when polishing a draft long-read assembly to final accuracy with Illumina short reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-read-error-correction))
- **nanopore-read-filtering-statistics-summarization**: Reference for generating or verifying per-sample output tables (reads.tsv, consensus.tsv, contigs.tsv) and HTML report sections summarizing read filtering statistics from a ViMOP Nextflow run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-filtering-statistics-summarization))
- **nanopore-read-haplotype-phasing**: Use when long nanopore reads must be assigned to a parental haplotype using trio-phased heterozygous SNPs, e.g. to phase SVs or determine parental origin of de novo breakpoint junctions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-haplotype-phasing))
- **nanopore-read-haplotype-tagging**: Use when ONT nanopore reads (e.g., an HG002 R10.4 alignment BAM) must be partitioned into paternal/maternal haplotype read sets for allele-specific methylation analysis and a phased VCF of heterozygous variants is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-haplotype-tagging))
- **nanopore-read-kmer-extraction**: Use when compressing or indexing ONT nanopore read sequences from a FASTQ file and a k-mer–based MinHash sketch is needed to find reads overlapping a query sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-kmer-extraction))
- **nanopore-read-length-statistics**: Use when characterizing a nanopore WGS dataset by read-length statistics and genome-wide coverage at MAPQ ≥ 20 across alternative aligners (e.g., rebuilding the NA19240 control metrics from Table 2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-length-statistics))
- **nanopore-read-library-configuration**: Use when preparing Fast-SG hybrid scaffolding or assembly runs on raw Nanopore/PacBio long-read FASTA libraries, e.g. shallow ~5X NA12878 ultra-long reads feeding a scaffolding graph built with k-mer size 22. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-library-configuration))
- **nanopore-read-mapping-benchmarking**: Use when comparing long-read mapper sensitivity (mapped reads/bases) or consensus accuracy on Oxford Nanopore MinION data against published GraphMap results, or when validating a mapper build on real MinION data sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-mapping-benchmarking))
- **nanopore-read-mapping-decision-scoring**: Use when you have a per-read feature table (read_id, mapping-quality ratio, chain-score ratio, mean chain-score ratio) from RawHash2 output and need to reproduce or audit the weighted-sum mapped/unmapped decision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-mapping-decision-scoring))
- **nanopore-read-mapping-filtering**: Use when raw nanopore DRS fast5 signals need transcript-level assignment — e.g. before curating training/testing sets for RISER, or before computing per-transcript read/nucleotide counts for enrichment or depletion benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-mapping-filtering))
- **nanopore-read-metadata-inspection**: Use when the task requires locating a subset of reads by read ID within one or more POD5 files — e.g. searching read IDs by substring, sorting them lexicographically, or intersecting them with a supplied list of read IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-metadata-inspection))
- **nanopore-read-phasing-with-snp-haplotyping**: Use when ONT reads need to be assigned to parental haplotypes to resolve haplotype-specific methylation — e.g. detecting imprinted control regions, parent-of-origin labeling in a trio, or differential methylation between phases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-phasing-with-snp-haplotyping))
- **nanopore-read-preprocessing-and-trimming**: Use when raw nanopore sequencing files (FASTQ, .fastq.gz, FAST5, or POD5) must be basecalled, preprocessed, and primer-trimmed before viral genome reconstruction or taxonomic classification in VirDetector. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-preprocessing-and-trimming))
- **nanopore-read-probability-statistical-testing**: Use when comparing nanopore direct RNA m6A between two conditions (e.g. WT vs knock-out replicates) using per-read modification probabilities to call differentially modified transcriptomic sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-probability-statistical-testing))
- **nanopore-read-processing**: Use when you have untargeted metagenomic nanopore FASTQ data (optionally barcode-demultiplexed into barcode01, barcode02, ... subdirectories) from MinION sequencing of clinical or animal samples and need to detect known viral species and reconstruct their consensus genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-processing))
- **nanopore-read-quality-filtering**: Use when running mCaller on ONT eventalign data and read-level noise from low-quality basecalls is degrading per-position methylation accuracy, and you want to restrict classification to reads above an average base-quality threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-quality-filtering))
- **nanopore-read-reference-alignment**: Use when a viral genome must be reconstructed from nanopore FASTQ reads for which a suitable reference FASTA already exists, e.g. a known virus such as West Nile virus where mapping beats de novo assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-reference-alignment))
- **nanopore-read-resistance-genotyping**: Use when you have raw single-molecule nanopore (MinION) reads of a bacterial isolate and need antibiotic resistance predictions without prior error correction, ideally benchmarked against a second sequencing platform. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-resistance-genotyping))
- **nanopore-read-simulation**: Use when evaluating SV-caller sensitivity/specificity (NanoSV, Lumpy, Sniffles) without wet-lab validation: simulated nanopore reads with a realistic error profile and known ground-truth breakpoints must be generated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-simulation))
- **nanopore-read-simulation**: Use when you need ground-truth nanopore read sets of known origin and adjustable accuracy to benchmark read classification or alignment accuracy (F1, precision, recall) and throughput. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-simulation))
- **nanopore-read-simulation-error-modeling**: Use when benchmarking long-read taxonomic classifiers or ARG host-assignment pipelines and realistic simulated ONT metagenomes with controlled error profiles (HQ vs LQ) and known ground-truth species composition are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-simulation-error-modeling))
- **nanopore-read-subsampling**: Use when you need to determine how many Nanopore amplicon reads are sufficient for an accurate majority-vote consensus, by sweeping subsample sizes and measuring consensus error against a higher-accuracy reference consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-subsampling))
- **nanopore-read-subsampling-coverage-normalization**: Use when pooled Nanopore FASTQs of uneven depth must be subsampled to a fixed coverage (e.g. 50x or 60x) before assembly benchmarking or fair tool comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-subsampling-coverage-normalization))
- **nanopore-read-subsampling-coverage-scaling**: Use when assessing the minimum nanopore sequencing depth at which nanoHiMe-seq CpG methylation calls remain accurate versus WGBS, e.g. before committing MinION flowcells for a low-coverage epigenomics experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-subsampling-coverage-scaling))
- **nanopore-read-taxonomic-classification**: Use when you have untargeted metagenomic nanopore FASTQ reads (e.g. Lassa, Ebola, Dengue clinical samples) and need to remove non-viral reads based on Centrifuge taxonomic classification confidence before viral genome reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-taxonomic-classification))
- **nanopore-read-to-seed-alignment**: Use when you have FASTA-formatted nanopore reads (e.g. R7.3, R9 1D/2D, R9.4 1D MinION runs) plus a set of pre-selected 'not too repetitive' seed sequences of uniform length, and need read-to-seed alignments to drive a TULIP seed-layout assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-to-seed-alignment))
- **nanopore-read-until-platform-comparison**: Use when reported per-read execution time (ms) and energy (mJ) measurements exist for HARU (Xilinx FPGA + 4-core ARM) and one or more software baselines, and the goal is to derive or verify EDP and platform-level efficiency ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-until-platform-comparison))
- **nanopore-read-variant-calling**: Use when nanopore reads (e.g. nanoHiMe-seq FASTQ/BAM aligned to hg19) must be assigned to parental alleles — e.g. to compute per-allele phasing rates over H3K4me3/H3K27me3 peaks or CpG islands — and heterozygous variants are available or need calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-read-variant-calling))
- **nanopore-reference-alignment-parameter-tuning**: Use when raw multi-fast5 ONT direct RNA reads must be converted to single-read fast5 and raw current signal aligned (resquiggled) to a reference FASTA before per-position modification detection, especially when tuning alignment/event-detection parameters for rRNA or similar references. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-reference-alignment-parameter-tuning))
- **nanopore-signal-basecalling-and-alignment**: Use when raw nanopore direct RNA sequencing fast5 files must be assigned transcript identities (e.g., to label RNA classes for model training or quantify transcript abundance) via basecalling, transcriptome mapping, and high-confidence alignment filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-basecalling-and-alignment))
- **nanopore-signal-binary-npy-export**: Use when a single read's raw nanopore current measurements stored in a POD5 file must be extracted into a .npy Numpy binary or plain-text file for downstream numpy processing without the pod5 API. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-binary-npy-export))
- **nanopore-signal-classification**: Use when raw nanopore DRS signals (fast5, RNA002 or RNA004 kits) must be classified into RNA classes such as protein_coding mRNA, mtRNA, lncRNA or globin (HBA1/HBA2/HBB) directly from the first few seconds of signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-classification))
- **nanopore-signal-event-alignment**: Use when starting from raw nanopore FAST5 files and you need event-level signal-to-reference alignments (test.eventalign.txt) as input for nanoHiMe modification calling or model training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-event-alignment))
- **nanopore-signal-event-extraction**: Use when raw ONT fast5 reads must be resquiggled, converted to multi-fast5, and parsed into position-anchored signal events to support k-mer-level comparisons of native vs WGA samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-event-extraction))
- **nanopore-signal-feature-extraction**: Use when raw Nanopore FAST5/POD5 reads have been basecalled and re-squiggled against a genome reference and per-base sequence-context plus raw-signal features are needed to train or run a methylation-calling model on cytosine sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-feature-extraction))
- **nanopore-signal-inference-benchmarking**: Use when you need to measure and compare inference/processing throughput (kbp/s) of raw MinION fast5 signal tools or basecalling pipelines across hardware tiers such as a 2GB Jetson Nano versus a high-end desktop. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-inference-benchmarking))
- **nanopore-signal-methylation-calling**: Use when raw ONT signal data (POD5 or FAST5) from R9.4.1 or R10.4.1 flowcells must be basecalled, aligned, and analyzed for CpG 5mC methylation at per-read or per-site resolution, or benchmarked against short-read/microarray ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-methylation-calling--83027b159a802888))
- **nanopore-signal-methylation-calling**: Use when you need to detect methylated CpG sites from basecalled Nanopore reads (FASTQ/BAM) plus raw FAST5 signal data on a CUDA GPU, and prioritize end-to-end execution time versus CPU-only tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-methylation-calling--4d2f7972677cae37))
- **nanopore-signal-methylation-prediction**: Use when you have nanopore DRS FAST5/eventaligned signals (e.g. HEK293 WT, HeLa, IVT controls) and need single-molecule m6A/m5C calls, site-level probabilities, or stoichiometry in any sequence context without a KO/control sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-methylation-prediction))
- **nanopore-signal-preprocessing**: Use when raw DRS FAST5 data has been basecalled (Guppy 4.0.11+), aligned (minimap2, samtools -F 232457), and eventaligned (Nanopolish --scale-events), and per-read 9-mer signal inputs are needed for CHEUI Model 1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-preprocessing))
- **nanopore-signal-preprocessing-adapter-polya-trimming**: Use when raw nanopore DRS fast5 signals (RNA002/R9.4.1) must have the adapter and poly(A) stall trimmed before basecalling (e.g., Chiron/Dorado) or before feeding fixed-length inputs to a signal classifier such as RISER. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-preprocessing-adapter-polya-trimming))
- **nanopore-signal-read-classification**: Use when input is raw ONT fast5 signal data (amplified or metagenomic 16S) and you need per-read target-species detection on inexpensive hardware without basecalling, e.g. MinION as a species detector. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-read-classification))
- **nanopore-signal-resquiggle**: Use when raw (uncorrected) Nanopore FAST5 reads must have their electrical signals aligned to genome-reference bases before signal-based methylation calling, e.g. prior to deepsignal_plant call_mods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-resquiggle))
- **nanopore-signal-resquiggling**: Use when raw nanopore fast5 signal must be aligned to a reference genome and reformatted to multi-fast5 before running methylation motif callers such as Snapper's sample-vs-WGA comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-resquiggling))
- **nanopore-signal-resquiggling-against-transcriptome**: Use when raw Nanopore direct RNA sequencing FAST5 files must be basecalled, re-squiggled, and aligned to a reference transcriptome to produce signal-mapped reads for downstream per-site feature extraction (e.g. RedNano m6A detection). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-resquiggling-against-transcriptome))
- **nanopore-signal-simulation**: Use when synthetic ONT R10.4.1 DNA or RNA004 direct-RNA read signals (POD5) are needed to benchmark basecalling accuracy, variant calling, assembly, or simulator fidelity without real sequencing runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-simulation))
- **nanopore-signal-zscore-normalization**: Use when preparing raw nanopore signal reads (e.g., BLOW5 format from R9.4 chemistry) for signal-domain alignment such as sDTW-based Read Until, where events must be normalized and cast to 16-bit fixed point before dispatch to an accelerator. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-signal-zscore-normalization))
- **nanopore-simulated-read-benchmarking**: Use when comparing untargeted nanopore virus genome pipelines for robustness to high mutation levels or evaluating recovery of partial genomes below 50% completeness on simulated read sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-simulated-read-benchmarking))
- **nanopore-snp-calling-and-polishing**: Use when assembling Nanopore R9/R10 diploid datasets with PECAT, when corrected reads alone give insufficient SNP calls for phasing, or when Nanopore assemblies require post-assembly polishing before evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-snp-calling-and-polishing))
- **nanopore-snp-detection-filtering**: Use when MinION amplicon or whole-genome reads (FASTA/FASTQ) must be mapped to a reference and candidate SNPs called with explicit coverage thresholds, e.g., targeted Plasmodium falciparum or EGFR amplicon resequencing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-snp-detection-filtering))
- **nanopore-squiggle-dtw-alignment-execution**: Use when raw nanopore signal data in BLOW5 format must be aligned to a reference genome in the signal (squiggle) domain for Read Until / adaptive sampling, especially when real-time performance on resource-constrained devices is the goal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-squiggle-dtw-alignment-execution))
- **nanopore-squiggle-mapping**: Use when raw nanopore signal reads in BLOW5 format must be aligned directly against a reference genome at the signal level for Read Until / adaptive sampling decisions, especially when basecalling is too slow or resource-intensive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-squiggle-mapping))
- **nanopore-structural-variant-calling**: Use when long-read PromethION/nanopore WGS reads must be turned into a merged SV call set (DEL/DUP/INS/INV/TRA) and when large CNVs &gt;50 kb or coverage-supported polishing of caller output is the goal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-structural-variant-calling))
- **nanopore-structural-variant-qc**: Use when a raw NanoSV VCF from nanopore data (e.g. NA12878 chr1) needs stringent post-calling filtering before downstream comparison, phasing, or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-structural-variant-qc))
- **nanopore-sv-call-set-qc**: Use when a raw NanoSV VCF from MinION long-read data (tens of thousands of SV calls) must be reduced to a high-confidence SV set and intermediate/final call counts must match published checkpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-sv-call-set-qc))
- **nanopore-sv-calling**: Use when calling structural variants (deletions, inversions, translocations) from long nanopore reads at medium coverage (11–16×), or when reproducing the NanoSV SV discovery workflow from Cretu Stancu et al. 2017. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-sv-calling))
- **nanopore-translocation-time-novelty-detection**: Use when ONT nanopore FAST5 reads (e.g. NA12878 / SRR15058166) must be converted to per-base translocation times and scored for non-B DNA novelty against motif-free B-DNA controls at an FDR-controlled level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/nanopore-translocation-time-novelty-detection))
- **nanopore-vs-bisulfite-benchmarking**: Use when comparing nanopore methylation calls to whole-genome BS-seq in repetitive or centromeric regions, to quantify the fraction of CHH sites profiled by each method at a minimum read-coverage threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-vs-bisulfite-benchmarking))
- **nanopore-vs-bisulfite-cross-platform-comparison**: Use when comparing CpG site coverage between nanopore modification-call outputs (tsv with read_id, chromosome, pos_in_strand, strand, methylation_rate) and matched BS-seq calls across matched human or plant whole-genome datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-vs-bisulfite-cross-platform-comparison))
- **nanopore-wgbs-cross-platform-benchmarking**: Use when you have per-site methylation frequency files from nanopore tools and matched WGBS frequencies and need to test whether sequence context (k-mer around CpG) explains nanopore-WGBS disagreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-wgbs-cross-platform-benchmarking))
- **nanopore-wgbs-methylation-correlation-analysis**: Use when nanopore per-site methylation frequency tables (augmented BED format) must be compared to matched WGBS methylation frequencies across coverage thresholds (1x–20x) to verify concordance stability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-wgbs-methylation-correlation-analysis))
- **nanopore-wgs-dataset-acquisition**: Use when a workflow needs the raw nanopore WGS reads (e.g. ENA accessions ERR2585112/ERR2585115 from PRJEB26791, NA19240 PromethION) as FASTQ input for alignment-based HLA-I/II prediction or similar long-read benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-wgs-dataset-acquisition))
- **nanopore-wgs-read-streaming**: Use when a whole-genome shotgun long-read FASTQ (e.g. Oxford Nanopore PromethION WGS) needs HLA class I/II allele prediction, and disk/time-efficient single-command streaming of aligner output into HLAminer is preferred over writing SAM files to disk. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanopore-wgs-read-streaming))
- **nanoprd**: Turn a product idea into an executable plan. Produces project context, a PRD, an architecture document, and a set of nanotasks - atomic, doable tasks, one user-observable behavior each, small enough for a coding agent to pick up and finish in a single pass. Runs a five-phase workflow with a hard approval gate between phases and writes every deliverable to disk. Use this when the user says: "plan a project", "I have an idea for an app", "write a PRD", "make a spec", "help me architect this", "break this down into tasks", "turn this into tickets", or asks for module specs or a handoff prompt fo… ([arinadi/nanoPRD](https://github.com/arinadi/nanoPRD/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoprd))
- **nanoprogress-rust**: nanoprogress terminal progress bar conventions — builder API, ticks, finalization, TTY/custom writers, thread sharing, and CLI output. Use when writing Rust CLI code that imports 'nanoprogress'. ([avbel/ai-skills](https://github.com/avbel/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoprogress-rust) · ★2)
- **nanopub-assertion-extractor**: Extract scientific assertions from a research paper and package each one as a standards-compliant nanopublication (TriG/RDF with head, assertion, provenance and publication-info named graphs), every assertion carrying a verbatim evidence quote and an exact source location (page, section, paragraph, sentence). Use when a task asks to read a paper or abstract and produce nanopublications, machine-readable RDF/Turtle/TriG assertions, claim decomposition with evidence binding, assertion-source comparison tables, or structured knowledge units from literature. Trigger words: nanopublication, assert… ([hehekevin/boerproduct](https://github.com/hehekevin/boerproduct/tree/HEAD/week4/reading-%E6%96%87%E7%8C%AE%E9%98%85%E8%AF%BB/product-arena-09-0e8befd4_product_arena_09_0e8befd4))
- **nanopub-forge**: 科研阅读与Nanopublication结构化：把论文段落拆成可独立判断真假的原子断言，绑定逐字证据片段、页码/段落/字符定位与来源元数据，输出可机读 result.json + assertions.jsonl。单命令确定性兜底，Agent 用 draft.json 增量增强；材料缺失、无法判断、异常也都诚实记录并 exit 0。 ([Richard-He-happy/bohr-p4-portfolio](https://github.com/Richard-He-happy/bohr-p4-portfolio/tree/HEAD/challenges/reading-13))
- **nanorc-create**: Creates a new nanorc syntax highlighting file for a given file type and adds it to src/Syntax/. Use when asked to add syntax highlighting for a new file type to the editor. Researches well-known file types autonomously and asks targeted questions when details cannot be determined. ([blakepell/Editor](https://github.com/blakepell/Editor/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/nanorc-create))
- **nanoresearch-experiment**: Generate a Python code skeleton from an experiment blueprint ([OpenRaiser/NanoResearch](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/NanoResearch/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoresearch-experiment) · ★1.3k · 3 copies)
- **nanoresearch-experiment**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/nanoresearch-experiment) · ★745)
- **nanoresearch-ideation**: Search academic literature and generate research hypotheses ([OpenRaiser/NanoResearch](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/NanoResearch/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoresearch-ideation) · ★1.3k · 4 copies)
- **nanoresearch-ideation**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/nanoresearch-ideation) · ★745 · 2 copies)
- **nanoresearch-planning**: Produce an experiment blueprint from a research hypothesis ([OpenRaiser/NanoResearch](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/NanoResearch/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoresearch-planning) · ★1.3k · 3 copies)
- **nanoresearch-planning**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/nanoresearch-planning) · ★745)
- **nanoresearch-writing**: Draft a LaTeX research paper from all previous stage outputs ([OpenRaiser/NanoResearch](https://github.com/OpenRaiser/NanoResearch/tree/HEAD/skills/nanoresearch-writing) · ★1.3k · 2 copies)
- **nanoreview-net**: Access chipset (SoC), phone, and CPU specifications and benchmarks from NanoReview ([antins-labs/SearchOS](https://github.com/antins-labs/SearchOS/tree/HEAD/searchos/skills/library/access/nanoreview-net) · ★415)
- **nanosecond-demonstration**: Make abstract concepts tangible through physical demonstrations, analogies, and artifacts that people can see, touch, and remember. ([sethmblack/paks-skills](https://github.com/sethmblack/paks-skills/tree/HEAD/nanosecond-demonstration) · ★10)
- **nanosensor-calibration-manager**: Nanosensor characterization skill for calibration, sensitivity analysis, and selectivity validation ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/specializations/domains/science/nanotechnology/skills/nanosensor-calibration-manager) · ★1.9k)
- **nanoshape-reactivity-profiling**: Use when validating nanoSHAPE per-read reactivity (from current or dwell signal) against SHAPE-MaP on an AcIm-modified RNA such as pri-miR-17~92, or when determining how few reads are needed for a stable reactivity profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/nanoshape-reactivity-profiling))

- **naobi-build**: Naobi SDLC Build phase rules for implementing one ticket. User-invoked as /naobi-sdlc:naobi-build, or read by orchestrate coders. ([mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc](https://github.com/mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/naobi-build))
- **naobi-design**: Naobi SDLC Design phase. Turns docs/ledger.yaml into the design report (chapters 0-11 plus traceability appendix), Mermaid diagrams chosen by rule, ADRs, GLOSSARY.md, CONSTRAINTS.md, a design system and a project CLAUDE.md, then runs the Gate 1 ledger check. User-invoked as /naobi-sdlc:naobi-design after naobi-grill. ([mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc](https://github.com/mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/naobi-design))
- **naobi-grill**: Naobi SDLC Discover phase. Interviews the owner in rounds of numbered questions, each with a recommended answer, until all 14 mandatory topics (problem to constraints) are resolved, N/A or deferred to a prototype, and records every answer verbatim plus a testable statement in docs/ledger.yaml. User-invoked as /naobi-sdlc:naobi-grill at the start of a new project or major feature. ([mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc](https://github.com/mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/naobi-grill))
- **naobi-plan**: Naobi SDLC Plan phase. After Gate 1, breaks the approved design and docs/ledger.yaml into tracer-bullet tickets with blocking edges, file ownership and acceptance criteria copied from the ledger, gets the owner's Gate 2 approval, publishes them to Plane (or local files), and links every requirement to its tickets. User-invoked as /naobi-sdlc:naobi-plan, or read by the orchestrate architect. ([mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc](https://github.com/mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/naobi-plan))
- **naobi-ship**: Naobi SDLC Ship phase for Coolify on a VPS with Backblaze B2 backups. Mode setup wires CI (GitHub Actions to GHCR), copies the ledger and floor checkers into the project, adds the /healthz Dockerfile, env template, monitoring and the owner-run server wizard. Mode release runs staging, smoke tests, the go/no-go report, a pre-migration backup and the production deploy only after the owner's explicit go. Mode drill runs a backup restore drill. User-invoked as /naobi-sdlc:naobi-ship. ([mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc](https://github.com/mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/naobi-ship))
- **naobi-verify**: Naobi SDLC Verify phase. Reviews a change against its ticket and the ledger by running the mechanical gates first (CONSTRAINTS.md commands, floor_check, ledger_check with tickets and tests required) and then reporting five separate axes - Spec, Standards, Security (ASVS 5.0, STRIDE), Test strength (mutation testing on the diff) and Definition of Done - with severity labels and an APPROVE or REQUEST_CHANGES verdict. User-invoked as /naobi-sdlc:naobi-verify, or read by the orchestrate reviewer. ([mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc](https://github.com/mrraihan29/naobi-sdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/naobi-verify))

Use when: "narrate", "explain before committing", "comprehension check", or before any commit of AI-generated code. Triggers: "narrate this", "explain what I built", "comprehension gate", "do I understand this". ([tslateman/skills](https://github.com/tslateman/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/writing/narrate) · ★1)
- **narrate**: Talk the user through what you're building and why, like a teammate sitting next to them, optionally out loud. Use when the user runs the narrate command or asks you to explain what you're doing as you work. ([Allouche-Adam/pair-narrator](https://github.com/Allouche-Adam/pair-narrator/tree/HEAD/skills/narrate))
- **narrate**: Tails one or more agent-output log files and uses Claude Haiku to maintain a running plain-English narration of what the agent is doing. Reads either Sandcastle's `.sandcastle/logs/*.log` text files (per-phase per-branch — implementer, reviewer-N, revise-N, planner, merger) or generic `events.jsonl` stream-json files, and writes a timestamped `narration.md` next to each source. Aggregate mode merges multiple sources into a single cross-run summary with phase tags. Use when the user runs `/narrate &lt;log-or-dir&gt;`, says "narrate this run", "summarize what the agent is doing", "watch this run for… ([aztena/skills](https://github.com/aztena/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/narrate))
